# Skills · S · part 26

805 entries, scatac-scrna-adt-data-preprocessing to scenario-planning-for-alternative-futures. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **scatac-scrna-adt-data-preprocessing**: Use when raw count matrices of scRNA-seq, scATAC-seq, and/or CITE-seq ADT data must be normalized and preprocessed before training or applying scButterfly cross-modality translators. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scatac-scrna-adt-data-preprocessing))
- **scatac-scrna-integration-label-transfer**: Use when scATAC-seq cells lack annotations and a matched or related scRNA-seq reference with known cell types is available, e.g. predicting PBMC cell types from a ~10k-cell scRNA reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/scatac-scrna-integration-label-transfer))
- **scatac-seq-accessible-peak-identification**: Use when scRNA-seq data must be paired with scATAC-seq peaks and TF motif scans to filter out non-relevant TFs and construct a base GRN parquet (e.g. Cusanovich2018_Spleen_peak_base_GRN_dataframe.parquet) for downstream scGATE logic-gate inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scatac-seq-accessible-peak-identification))
- **scatac-seq-activity-matrix-preparation**: Use when raw scATAC-seq peak data has already been converted to a gene activity matrix (e.g. via Signac) and must be TF-IDF transformed onto scRNA-seq common genes before scBridge label transfer. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scatac-seq-activity-matrix-preparation))
- **scatac-seq-base-grn-parsing-and-summarization**: Use when a base GRN built from scATAC-seq data and TF binding motif analysis (a .parquet file of candidate TF–target links, e.g. Cusanovich2018_Spleen or Buenrostro2018) must be loaded and summarized before running scGATE on scRNA-seq counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scatac-seq-base-grn-parsing-and-summarization))
- **scatac-seq-cell-type-prediction**: Use when both a labeled scATAC-seq reference dataset (gene score matrix + cell-type metadata) and an unlabeled target scATAC-seq dataset are available and per-cell cell-type labels must be transferred without joint reference-target integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/scatac-seq-cell-type-prediction))
- **scatac-seq-celltyping-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking cell-typing predictions for scATAC-seq data — e.g., comparing Cellcano's predicted labels against FACS-derived ground truth for a human PBMC target dataset to quantify per-type accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/scatac-seq-celltyping-benchmark-evaluation))
- **scatac-seq-chromvar-tf-activity-scoring**: Use when validating DICE-predicted primed cell states and their candidate regulatory factors (e.g. Runx2, Zfhx3, Foxo3, Lmo7) in matched scATAC-Seq profiles, by testing whether motif-derived TF activity is higher in primed versus unprimed cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scatac-seq-chromvar-tf-activity-scoring))
- **scatac-seq-fragment-quality-control**: Use when starting an scATAC-seq analysis from a fragments file (e.g. BMMC dataset) and low-quality cells, doublets, and high-dimensional tile/peak matrices must be filtered and reduced before clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scatac-seq-fragment-quality-control))
- **scatac-seq-gene-accessibility-quantification**: Use when scRNA-Seq-derived cell assignments or marker gene lists must be validated against matched scATAC-Seq data by testing whether target genes of lineage regulators (e.g. GATA1, KLF1, IRF8) show higher chromatin accessibility in the predicted cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scatac-seq-gene-accessibility-quantification))
- **scatac-seq-gene-activity-inference**: Use when the input is scATAC-seq data and pseudo-space reconstruction is needed but no spatial ATAC-seq reference is available, requiring conversion to a gene activity matrix (via Signac) so ST data can serve as the spatial reference in scSpace. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scatac-seq-gene-activity-inference))
- **scatac-seq-gene-activity-matrix-analysis**: Use when input is a scATAC-seq dataset converted to a cell-by-gene activity score matrix and the goal is cell clustering and marker-gene identification via differential expression of the resulting clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scatac-seq-gene-activity-matrix-analysis))
- **scatac-seq-gene-activity-matrix-conversion**: Use when integrating paired or unpaired scRNA-seq and scATAC-seq data (e.g. the 10X Genomics PBMC multi-omic dataset) with tools that operate on gene-by-cell matrices rather than peak matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scatac-seq-gene-activity-matrix-conversion))
- **scatac-seq-gene-score-analysis**: Use when preparing a reference scATAC-seq gene score matrix (with known cell type labels) for supervised celltyping and you must reduce thousands of genes to a compact, informative feature set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/scatac-seq-gene-score-analysis))
- **scatac-seq-latent-feature-clustering**: Use when the input is a raw scATAC-seq count matrix (h5ad, txt/tsv/csv, or mtx folder with count/peak/barcode files) and the goal is cell-type clustering, visualization, or denoising/imputation with benchmarked accuracy against scABC, scVI, cisTopic, and SC3. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/scatac-seq-latent-feature-clustering))
- **scatac-seq-motif-activity-analysis**: Use when sparse scATAC-seq peak-by-cell data (h5ad) needs transcription factor motif activity scoring, especially to test whether SCALE-imputed data recovers more cluster-specific motifs than the raw matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/scatac-seq-motif-activity-analysis))
- **scatac-seq-peak-accessibility-processing**: Use when a scATAC-seq peak-by-cell count matrix must be reduced to an informative peak subset before clustering or trajectory inference, and standard HVF selection is suspected to obscure cell type structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scatac-seq-peak-accessibility-processing))
- **scatac-seq-peak-calling-benchmarking**: Use when benchmarking scATAC-seq peak callers against a known set of ground-truth peaks (e.g. scReadSim synthetic BAM from the sci-ATAC-seq dataset), or when choosing a peak caller for single-cell ATAC data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scatac-seq-peak-calling-benchmarking))
- **scatac-seq-peak-dropping-corruption**: Use when you need to quantify how a scATAC-seq clustering method's accuracy (ARI) degrades as sparsity increases, by simulating missingness through random peak dropping on a labeled dataset such as Forebrain (2088 cells, k=8). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/scatac-seq-peak-dropping-corruption))
- **scatac-seq-peak-matrix-export**: Use when after completing peak calling and cell annotation in an ArchR project, when you intend to perform trajectory analysis using STREAM rather than ArchR's native monocle3 or Slingshot integrations, or when you need to share peak-by-cell matrices with collaborators using STREAM pipelines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/scatac-seq-peak-matrix-export))
- **scatac-seq-peak-plus-te-feature-construction**: Use when analyzing 10x scATAC-seq (or multi-omics) data and you want clustering/benchmarking feature matrices that combine accessible-chromatin peaks with locus-specific TE counts from MATES, alongside TE-only and multi-mapping-TE-only perspectives. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scatac-seq-peak-plus-te-feature-construction))
- **scatac-seq-read-downsampling-simulation**: Use when evaluating scATAC-seq clustering methods for coverage robustness: simulated datasets at graded depths (1,000–10,000 reads/cell) are needed and cell-type recovery is quantified by ARI/NMI against reference labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/scatac-seq-read-downsampling-simulation))
- **scatac-seq-te-accessibility-quantification**: Use when scATAC-seq BAM files (e.g. FACS-purified mouse cell samples) should be characterized by TE open-chromatin content alone, to test whether TE accessibility recovers known cell identities or reveals TE-specific openness. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scatac-seq-te-accessibility-quantification))
- **scatac-tfidf-lsi-preprocessing**: Use when preparing scATAC-seq data (binary cell-by-bin accessibility matrix) to feed into cross-modality integration (e.g., with scRNA-seq via uniPort's coupled-VAE) alongside log-normalized gene expression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scatac-tfidf-lsi-preprocessing))
- **scatc-topic-modeling-on-scatc-seq-chromatin-accessibility**: Use when analyzing scATAC/SNARE-seq chromatin accessibility as a cell-by-peak matrix (e.g. ~31,758 cells x 575,265 peaks) and you need unsupervised topics and clusters in the chromatin modality before assigning cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scatc-topic-modeling-on-scatc-seq-chromatin-accessibility))

It specifically probes model robustness to camouflage, occlusion, varying lighting conditions, and small object detection in outdoor urban environments. - **scd-architecture**: 在生产实施前设计或验证领域模型、系统边界、数据所有权和共享的机器可读接口契约。适用于产品核心稳定后的 0 到 1 产品；新增服务、模块、公共 API、事件或集成；涉及数据、权限、事务、并发、兼容性、迁移、回滚或可靠性的重要决策；现有仓库的架构演进；或明确的架构、领域模型或接口设计请求。与 scd-discovery、scd-uiux 和 scd-quickdev 组合使用。不适用于清晰的局部实现、已受现有契约约束的简单端点、纯 UI/UX 工作，也不负责生产编码、迁移执行和部署。 ([mindcarver/thinloop](https://github.com/mindcarver/thinloop/tree/HEAD/skills/scd-architecture))
- **scd-discovery**: 在实施前，把定义不足的产品想法转化为经过明确确认且可测试的产品契约或交付契约。默认用于新产品、应用、插件、服务或系统；含多个相互依赖产品决策的重要功能；或数据所有权、权限、不可逆行为、公共接口、外部集成仍未确定的工作。已确认的全新产品会得到轻量 `.scd/product/prd.md`；清晰的孤立变更和缺陷按计划确认后使用Issue。也用于用户明确要求澄清、质疑或定义产品想法的情形。不适用于结果、边界和验收路径均清晰的局部变更；一个孤立歧义无需完整 Discovery 即可澄清。已确认结果跨越多个可独立验证的交付时，把项目拆解交给 scd-project，不要强制塞入一个过大的 Issue。 ([mindcarver/thinloop](https://github.com/mindcarver/thinloop/tree/HEAD/skills/scd-discovery))
- **scd-evolve**: 诊断一段已完成或暂停的开发交互，并对真实使用过的 Thinloop 技能提出一项经过证据分级的改进。只有用户明确要求根据当前对话或提供的转录演进、改进或自优化 Thinloop，或明确确认实施此前提出的演进候选项时使用。 ([mindcarver/thinloop](https://github.com/mindcarver/thinloop/tree/HEAD/skills/scd-evolve))
- **scd-execute**: 消费 scd-project 的实时 Delivery-Issue DAG、选择当前安全 READY 波次，并把每个 Issue 委派给一条隔离的 scd-quickdev 通道，从而执行已确认的 Thinloop Initiative。适用于用户要求开始、继续、恢复或完成已确认的多交付项目，运行其当前 READY Issues，或执行其 DAG。除非用户要求更窄或串行波次，默认在可用并发范围内选择所有可安全独立执行的 READY 节点。安全时并行开发，逐个合并符合条件的拉取请求，并在每次交付状态变化后重新计算依赖图。不适用于单个 Issue、项目拆解、未确认或陈旧依赖图，也不能作为持久调度器。 ([mindcarver/thinloop](https://github.com/mindcarver/thinloop/tree/HEAD/skills/scd-execute))
- **scd-interview**: 根据明确请求评审当前对话，提取值得保存供个人学习的面试题；每题包含参考答案、标签、难度和来源；展示候选项供确认后，把已确认题目写入个人跨项目题库；也可按请求检索和浏览已保存题目。只有用户明确要求提取、保存、评审、搜索或管理面试题时使用。普通开发期间不得自动调用。 ([mindcarver/thinloop](https://github.com/mindcarver/thinloop/tree/HEAD/skills/scd-interview))
- **scd-knowledge**: 把有证据支持的开发经验沉淀为简洁、经过确认的项目 Markdown 知识或个人跨项目 Markdown 知识，并按请求检索、更新、合并或归档。只有用户明确要求沉淀、评审、保存、搜索、更新或管理开发经验或知识时使用。普通开发期间不得自动调用。 ([mindcarver/thinloop](https://github.com/mindcarver/thinloop/tree/HEAD/skills/scd-knowledge))
- **scd-maintenance**: 审计并修复仓库技术债务、代码文档漂移、陈旧规格、架构侵蚀、死代码、依赖卫生和过时项目产物。适用于用户明确要求检查、清理、统一、现代化或减少现有仓库债务，包括‘审计这个仓库’、‘找陈旧文档’、‘清理技术债’或‘让实施与文档一致’。普通功能开发、例行代码评审或文档编辑期间不得自动调用；项目级重构或重新实施应使用 scd-reengineering。 ([mindcarver/thinloop](https://github.com/mindcarver/thinloop/tree/HEAD/skills/scd-maintenance))
- **scd-next**: 检查当前仓库的实时项目状态并建议唯一下一行动。适用于用户询问哪些工作已完成、进行中、未完成、READY、PLANNED 或受阻；询问 Issue、拉取请求、Initiative 或里程碑状态；或不知道如何继续或恢复。根据可用性读取 GitHub Issues、拉取请求、Initiative DAG、验收证据、分支、工作树和本地 Thinloop 状态。既适用于多 Issue Initiatives，也适用于普通 Issue 管理的仓库。默认保持只读：不创建或编辑 Issues、不修改依赖图、不开始实施、不合并，也不编造优先级、完成百分比或验收。 ([mindcarver/thinloop](https://github.com/mindcarver/thinloop/tree/HEAD/skills/scd-next))
- **scd-project**: 在实施前，把跨越多个可独立验证交付的已确认项目转化为 GitHub Initiative 和 Issue 级依赖 DAG。适用于一个请求需要多个 Delivery Issues、硬前置依赖、滚动式项目拆解或明确集成门的情形。产品结果或共享规则仍未确定时，先使用 scd-discovery。不适用于一项连贯交付、一个完整 Issue，也不能仅因实施规模大而使用。本技能创建或更新项目与交付 Issues、验证依赖图，并报告 READY、BLOCKED、PLANNED 和 DONE 节点；它不实施代码、不启动 Agent、不创建工作树、不合并拉取请求，也不运行执行循环。 ([mindcarver/thinloop](https://github.com/mindcarver/thinloop/tree/HEAD/skills/scd-project))
- **scd-quickdev**: 编码 Agent 被要求修改仓库时，把清晰的单交付仓库变更从中文 Issue 推进到实现、验证、独立验收、PR 合并与清理。适用于缺陷、功能、配置、迁移或恢复；只执行明确选中的 READY Delivery Issue。需求未定用 scd-discovery，多交付拆分用 scd-project，状态查询用 scd-next。 ([mindcarver/thinloop](https://github.com/mindcarver/thinloop/tree/HEAD/skills/scd-quickdev))
- **scd-reengineering**: 通过有证据支持的项目级重构或新实施，对现有仓库进行再工程；可以改变语言、框架、架构、存储或运行时，但只保留明确选中的行为和兼容性。适用于重写、重新实施、替换、移植、现代化或大幅重构开源或内部项目，且工作跨越多个可独立验证交付。固定来源和许可证证据，建立可执行基线，定义保留/改变/删除边界，组合 scd-discovery、scd-architecture、scd-project、scd-execute 和 scd-quickdev，并以有界并行执行已确认 READY Issue 波次。不适用于小型局部重构、普通维护、没有来源基线的新产品，或未确认的生产切换。 ([mindcarver/thinloop](https://github.com/mindcarver/thinloop/tree/HEAD/skills/scd-reengineering))
- **scd-research**: 在产品实施前研究目标用户、需求强度、竞品与替代方案、采用阻力和关键假设，帮助决定继续、收窄、调整或暂缓。适用于新产品缺乏关键机会证据，或明确要求产品前期调研、用户研究、竞品分析、判断需求是否真实。可独立交付研究；需要定义产品时交给 scd-discovery。不用于通用新闻/学术检索、清晰局部变更、已有充分依据的批准规格或生产实施。 ([mindcarver/thinloop](https://github.com/mindcarver/thinloop/tree/HEAD/skills/scd-research))
- **scd-test**: 根据用户原始要求和已批准版本设计可执行测试，或独立验收功能、差异和 PR。适用于测试方案、回归场景、证据缺口、核对原要求是否兑现。只问交付进度用 scd-next；要求实际修改产品或持久测试代码由已获批的 scd-quickdev 交付。 ([mindcarver/thinloop](https://github.com/mindcarver/thinloop/tree/HEAD/skills/scd-test))
